### **Lead Stages Covered in Exact CRM Sequence (17 Stages per Project):**
1. **New Inquiry** – *Instant acknowledgment of fresh lead, building immediate trust and inviting to show flat.*
2. **testing** – *System test broadcast to verify SMS gateway, dynamic tag delivery, and contact validation.*
3. **Deactivate Lead** – *Dormancy notification that stops repeated reminders while leaving a positive, dignified impression.*
4. **Duplicate** – *Consolidating duplicate lead inquiries under a single dedicated Relationship Manager.*
5. **Follow Up** – *Value-driven follow up highlighting limited premium inventory and construction milestones.*
6. **Not Interested** – *Polite acknowledgment when customer says not interested, preserving future goodwill.*
7. **Call Back** – *Professional confirmation of requested call back at customer's convenient time.*
8. **Low budget** – *Addressing budget concerns with flexible construction-linked payment plans and pre-approved home loans.*
9. **Postponed** – *Nurturing delayed decision-makers with price escalation updates and holding preferential benefits.*
10. **Visit Schedule** – *Confirming scheduled site visit with clear date, time, Google Map pin, valet, and host contact.*
11. **Constant NR** – *Re-engaging non-responsive leads with high-curiosity video walkthrough link and 1-click reply.*
12. **Site Visit Done** – *Post-visit gratitude note sharing digital brochure, unit plans, and transparent price breakdown.*
13. **Revisit Schedule** – *Confirming repeat visit with key family decision-makers to shortlist specific units.*
14. **Revisit Done** – *Post-revisit closing recap offering 48-hour temporary unit reservation before general release.*
15. **Negotiation** – *Extending management-approved spot-booking commercial privileges with limited validity.*
16. **Lost** – *Courteous, dignified farewell when deal is lost, congratulating customer on their purchase elsewhere.*
17. **Won** – *Celebratory grand welcome to new homeowner, sharing onboarding roadmap and relationship manager contact.*

**Approved DLT Header (Sender ID):** `SHSIMP`

- Strategic High-Appreciation Location: Direct frontage on SP Ring Road connecting Nikol, Naroda & Airport
